# Chapter 001 · 2025-06-18 · The Adventure Starts

Date: 2025-06-18 09:00:00
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/the-adventure-starts/

My children, the season is changing. The summer will be a hot one. I am turning up the heat.

Ground zero, you will hear this in your news.

Autumn comes bringing good things.

Much is at stake. A lot is on the table. There will be NO nuclear war. False flags are appearing but nothing to fear. My David will deal with Iran's leader. He won't miss. Something beautiful arises from this. A stream will appear in the desert. Fresh water to declare a refreshing. A fountain of My glory will be seen. I'm bringing healing on My wings. There is much to be thankful for. I am doing a new thing in that region. Much joy and praise will be seen. I'm making all things new.

A weather report will come out soon declaring a typhoon. The Pacific has one coming. Look around Indonesia.

Marxist, you will hear this word in the coming days.

Revolution, this word will be heard too.

As the season changes so will your news. Things will go from bad to worse. I am your Anchor in the storm. I am here for you to hold on to. My fingers never lose their grip. I will hold you up through all of this. I am a promise keeper. You can count on Me. I am on a rescue mission says the LORD GOD.

Much is happening on Capitol Hill. Many discoveries will be found. Exposure after exposure is coming out. Heads will spin. I mean business. The lies, division and hate must go. All those working against My David will be found out. A day of reckoning is here. They will face the consequences of their actions. No holds barred. It's time to get this done.

Stock up on some food supplies before July. Things will be hard to get to for awhile. This won't last long but I want you prepared. Grocery stores will be shut down for a time. Stock up on a few weeks of water and supplies. This will pass in no time. They'll try to shut down your food supply. Trucks won't be able to deliver for the riots and chaos. I have manna from heaven so don't worry about what you will eat. Stay locked into Me. I am your nourishment.

A planned attack is coming from the left. It will be brutal at first but they don't get what they want. My Angel Armies are standing by. This doesn't go well for the evil. It will be a blood bath. My power will be seen with force. I am the GOD of the universe and the Protector of My people. I won't let anything harm those in My SON. My words are faithful and true. There is a lot going on but nothing I can't handle. Just press into Me. Rejoice and sing for you are watching your victory. I am breaking every chain. I am setting you free says the LORD GOD ALMIGHTY.

The left have a plan they are about to execute. I will let it go just far enough to bring them out in the open, then you will watch them fall as their plans come to nothing.

Keep praying against this evil trying to take you out. Keep praising, worshipping and shouting the enemy down. They won't know what hit them. I'll lay them all down flat. They do not recover from this.

A motor club will be in your news.

A rocket will be too. This is no ordinary thing. It's special, pay attention.

Starbucks is getting a make-over. Their woke agenda is dying. They'll fashion a new design to fit in with the publics likes.

A well known shoe maker is about to make headlines. This does some major damage to their brand.

A mushroom factory in east Texas is about to have an explosion.

Open borders will be a thing of the past. New legislation will make sure of that. No more radical terrorist will be allowed in. Tight scrutiny will be put into place.

Things are about to take place that will be put in history books. This is a historic shift in your nation. A new thing is being born.

You have access into My presence. Come and join Me. Let Me fill you with peace. I am always here waiting for time with you. You are My treasure and I am your Reward. I have so much to give you if you will let Me. I am opening doors no man can shut and closing doors no man can open. I'm ushering in a new age where prosperity and growth will be seen. You will grow exponentially. THE LAND OF THE FREE, THE HOME OF THE BRAVE. You'll be leading the way in the world stage. You are about to make history, My children. Trust in all I am doing says the LORD of HOST your Redeemer.

““Whatever I tell you in the dark, speak in the light; and what you hear in the ear, preach on the housetops. And do not fear those who kill the body but cannot kill the soul. But rather fear Him who is able to destroy both soul and body in hell. Are not two sparrows sold for a copper coin? And not one of them falls to the ground apart from your Father’s will. But the very hairs of your head are all numbered. Do not fear therefore; you are of more value than many sparrows.”

# Chapter 005 · 2026-05-20 · Get Ready, There Is So Much More

Date: 2026-05-20 17:00:05
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=https://youtu.be/AH3VxXxiDWI
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/get-ready-there-is-so-much-more/

My children, the timer is going off. Time to wake up out of your slumber. The bell is ringing. Hear the sound? The event is about to happen. You know, the one you’ve been waiting for. No, I haven’t forgotten. In fact I’ve been moving forward. I have plans set. I have demolition and rebuilding to do. I am making you new. It will be like moving into your first new home. Everything smells fresh and never been used. That is what your new life will feel like. It will be a new beginning with so many fresh opportunities. You’ll be walking on shine. Yes, I just gave My daughter that song in her head. I know all the songs you remember.

A future so bright, so much different. You won’t miss this way of living. It will soon be a memory you tell your children and grand children. It won’t be hard to leave behind. You’ll move forward with excitement and expectation. Yes, expect for these things to be yours in an instant. The wealth transfer is soon to happen. Soon you’ll be singing and dancing.

You see there are many ways you’ll receive the wealth. One is from the pharmaceutical companies and the poison they’ve been given you. But the rabbit hole goes much further. Cancer will soon be exposed. There are many ways to treat it without all the deadly poison and death elixirs. It’s all been about money and destroying your health to take control. Control they’ll have no more! The hole goes much deeper than you can imagine. They make back door deals with the food industry to poison your daily meals. But the harm doesn’t stop there. They pay many others especially in the plastic industry to cause harm too. So you see, they lose it all. You get part of your wealth transfer and I will restore your health with My healing from heaven. All the enemy has stolen will be returned. Behind every corner was a deal to make you sick. All these companies will pay back every penny. This is just one way I’m bringing your wealth. It will come quickly for some and slower for others so don’t lose heart. I am giving all My children more than they dreamed.

Tick, tock, tick tock, The clock is striking 12. 12 is the start of everything changing. Your health will be restored. Your vats will be overflowing. My glory will fill this earth. A transition and a new birth is taking place. Nothing will stay the same.

So you can see the light at the end of the tunnel. Your waiting is almost over. I’ve been on a stealth mission. I’ve been operating and shifting things in place to bring in My GLORY DAYS!

You are transitioning into something new. The mountains obey My words. All of heaven is in awe as I bring in this new age. The GOLDEN AGE is about to start. Brace for impact. You’ll feel the shift. It’s time to bring My promises in says the LORD of HOSTS your Redeemer!

# Chapter 008 · 2026-05-25 · Make Way for Change

Date: 2026-05-25 12:45:26
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=https://youtu.be/8ohqwETP0XU
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/make-way-for-change/

My children, let your light shine for all the world to see. Shine bright through the dark days ahead. Be a beacon, be a friend. People will be hurting and confused. Bring them comfort with good news. Tell them all this was said through the prophets and the bright future they have waiting. When they see your joy, you singing and dancing they will follow. They will say “ they were right about the Great and Terrible Day so they must be right about the future ahead. “ There is hope for tomorrow. Although you have a long summer of tribunals and trials, a summer of taking back your government from the elites lurking in the shadows. You are taking back what is yours. All that was stolen will be returned.

Your financial system is about to change. I’m bringing it down and bringing in better. A gold and silver standard. You will see the current system transition. Crypto will play an important part. My David is making things safe and reliable. No more worry about your dollar. A new system is being birthed. It holds all you need to succeed in the future. A future so bright you’ll need shades. I’m taking you higher, My children.

Higher, higher than you’ve ever known. Higher than you could imagine. You will be flying around from place to place in your futuristic cars. You’ll be growing a system that is steady and sure. Your children will never know or live like you did in bondage. They get the future that I promised.

So buckle up your saddles and put on your rhinestones. The rodeo is about to start. It’s time to let the bulls out. It’s time to bring this circus tent down and erect another way of living. I’m fulfilling all your hopes and dreams. I’m birthing in a new season.

Change, change so much change. It will start to accelerate after summer. So position yourselves for greatness! Help is on the way. I’m sending My angels to make a way. So much is about to take place. You are on the precipice of something great.

Your satellight system will be down for a time. This is after the Great and Terrible Day. No phones or internet for about two weeks. You know what to do. Just trust in Me. I’ll get you through.

A wolf, a wolf is about to be exposed. I’m leading him into a trap of My own. He is giving intel on My David for another assassination attempt but this too will fail and cage him in. My David is protected by Me. The death angel will take care of the wolves circling him now. No more will they have a chance.

This is it! The time is now! You are about to feel the sonic boom! Hunker down and be prepared. The death angel is about to make his rounds. We are in this together, you and I. I hold you tightly in My grip. Nothing will happen to My elect. I am your Keeper and Protector. You are safe with Me, My children says the LORD of HOSTS your Redeemer!

“Love one another with brotherly affection [as members of one family], giving precedence and showing honor to one another. Never lag in zeal and in earnest endeavor; be aglow and burning with the Spirit, serving the Lord. Rejoice and exult in hope; be steadfast and patient in suffering and tribulation; be constant in prayer. Contribute to the needs of God's people [sharing in the necessities of the saints]; pursue the practice of hospitality. Bless those who persecute you [who are cruel in their attitude toward you]; bless and do not curse them. Rejoice with those who rejoice [sharing others' joy], and weep with those who weep [sharing others' grief].”

# Chapter 011 · 2026-05-28 · A Time of Renewal

Date: 2026-05-28 10:17:30
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=https://youtu.be/99Qe2ORsTSI
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/a-time-of-renewal/

My children, it’s about to start. Hold on tight. The death angel is about to strike. 1,2,3,4 You are walking through the door. No more, no more will this evil ruin My earth. It’s time to take back what is yours. It’s time for a new birth.

There is no time like the present to get things done. I woke up My daughter to Ezekiel 12:22-26. I will tarry no longer. So fasten your hearts to the day at hand. Be My vessels through it all. I have equipped you for this time. I have prepared and warned you and given ample time for you to walk through this day of vengeance I am releasing on the wicked. No more will you be troubled by them. No more will they be here. No more!

My vengeance is kindled and the cup of wrath is overflowing. I vowed to destroy them all. Not one will be left. So break out in singing and dancing. Your freedom is here. Rejoice, rejoice for your new life. You are walking in to the other side. A land of milk and honey where all My promise are waiting to be fulfilled. My glory will fill this earth. You will have revival go on for days. So many hearts will come to Me. Repentance, so much repentance there will be when the GREAT I AM makes you free!

You are anointed and appointed for this time. I chose you to bring in the new era, a new birth to make you new. You are My chosen elect. I have great days prepared ahead. You will walk in the anointing that is heaven sent. The Holy Spirit will fill you all. You will celebrate your fresh start. Families will come together not grow apart. The wayward child will return home. There will be no feeble among you. No more woke, no more frequencies that alter the mind. You will have a fresh start as I heal you all. No more worry, no more doubt. You will rely on Me all the time. I am your Save Haven.

There is clean up that will need to be done, My children. Walk in love. Love covers all things. So enjoy this time of transition and change. You are going on an adventure like a little child. Everything will be new and exciting.

My glory will fall like rain. Heaven is coming down. Take a look around. This will all be gone. You have a whole new Make-over coming from Yours Truly.

Do not fret. Don’t be alarmed. Just go with the flow walking in harmony. Let all things be done without selfish ambition. Love your neighbor and be a friend. Be ambassadors for My SON. Be kingdom minded. All things are yours. It’s about to be all you ever dreamed and more says the LORD of HOST your Redeemer!

# Chapter 012 · 2026-05-29 · The Pains of Birth

Date: 2026-05-29 09:00:06
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=https://youtu.be/61bf2a5oAnc
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/the-pains-of-birth/

My children, I hold you dear in My heart. You are always in My thoughts. I have an everlasting love for you all. Don’t ever doubt that. Know there is nothing you could do to take that away. You are being changed. I am renewing in you a steadfast spirit prepared for righteousness. You are on the edge about to step into your new beginning. All the pressure and pains of your new birth are about to be over. You will soon be rejoicing, rejoicing for all the promises coming to pass. Rejoicing because you finally made it through the birthing canal and your new life is about to start. All I have stored up for you is more than you can comprehend. I have been waiting for this moment to bring it all in. I have been on a stealth mission. I have been working in secret. The enemy is in for a big surprise. One that will stop them in their tracks. They are squirming in fear. They sense the end is near. They think eliminating My David will stop what is coming for them but they are sadly mistaken. Nothing can stop what I am about to do and My David is anointed to bring this new age in. He dreams of it every night. He wants a future for you America. He wants you to prosper and lead the world into greatness.

The Pope, this is his final round. Much change will take place in the places of religion. I am turning it all around in this season. You think when the pope is no more the end will be but you are mistaken and forget what My hands can forge in the fire. I am bringing in new wine. Religious chains are about to be broke off by My amazing grace. How sweet the sound of freedom and a life set free!

My children, you walk in power and authority You walk in a powerful anointing that strips the enemy bare of any weapons used against you. My daughters’ story won’t disappoint you when she tells how she began to walk in the power and authority in my name and how her life changed and the enemy was brought to nothing. But that is another story for another time.

You have to believe it to receive it. If you doubt your tossed around, a wavering soul. By faith grab ahold of all you hold. Might, power and strength are found when you walk in the authority I give you through My SON’s death. You truly do reign as kings and priest. You posses it all!

My children, you will see much fear, pain and anguish once the death angel strikes. It grieves My heart greatly but it has to be done for the new to come and the old to fade. Give them all hope for a new day. Your future is so bright you’ll need to wear shades.

Are you buckled in? It’s time to start. It will be over before you know it but it will be the longest 24 hours you ever endured. It will be like nothing ever before or ever again. It is the day of reckoning.

You are My body. I cherish you all. We get through this together, You and I says the LORD of HOSTS your Redeemer!

# Chapter 014 · 2026-05-31 · Fingers and Toes: The Answers You’ve Been Waiting For

Date: 2026-05-31 13:46:06
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=https://youtu.be/MQ4nV5G2OPI
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/fingers-and-toes-the-answers-youve-been-waiting-for/

My children, the road ahead is long but this is near. You have a hope and a future. You have My promises I have given. You are stepping over to the other side full of excitement and wonder. Full of all your hopes and dreams. You are My vessels of righteousness to bring in this new age.

People are asking My daughter where in My word does it mention the Golden Age. Let Me ask you this, where in My word does it actually mention My church age?There were plenty of prophecy about My SON but the Church Age itself was never mentioned. They went through a transition just like you are about to now. The Holy Spirit fell and revealed it all. So will it be in this transition. So sit tight and pay attention.

There will be much travail when the death angel strikes. It’s not today but soon. That doesn’t mean I am not giving My time analyst revelation through My word and dates as clues. He is being obedient in all I show him. These are important markers to remember and he is a powerful weapon in throwing off the enemy.

My daughter asked if great repentance would come after the Great and Terrible Day. I told her some but not what you think. This is judgement and people don’t like when I use My wrath. There will be anger I interrupted peoples summer. There will be sorrow for the much death that will occur. Some, yes a few thousand will get on their knees and repent but this devastation is going to be difficult for most to accept.

I told My daughter to address the satellites and loss of service. She told you what I told her. There will be loss of cell phones and internet service but it is for your benefit. This is important for the reset. I am cleansing your nation and will cut off all communication for the enemies embedded deep. You’ll come back online with a message from your president. You will be rejoicing and singing. You rededicated this nation back to Me. I will set it all in order. So yes, do not panic. It is for your own good. Be prepared for things to be different. Schools won’t start back up until September. You are transitioning over summer. Expect some delay but you will have a much better system and design.

So where are we on the time line? Count your fingers and toes. It’s somewhere in the middle of those. It’s close. Ask Me before you make plans. This week will be safe and sound. I want you to enjoy your family and plans. But check back with Me next week on this day. You’ll be counting down and it’s very close. Pray to Me where ever you go.

Thank you for showing My daughter much love. Her heart is dear. She loves you all. I did keep her in the wilderness and out of sight until the right time.

All My plans are coming together. Keep the faith and don’t fret. Your new life is just ahead says the LORD of HOSTS your Redeemer!

“Now a great sign appeared in heaven: a woman clothed with the sun, with the moon under her feet, and on her head a garland of twelve stars. Then being with child, she cried out in labor and in pain to give birth.”

The summer that brought in righteousness.

NATO is corrupt to the core. Its nephilim leaders will soon be no more. A new thing will take front and center. An Allegiance of Nations will begin. A new way done in righteousness will be born. The old will be gone. You are starting over says the LORD of HOST your Redeemer!

“Finally, all [of you] should be of one and the same mind (united in spirit), sympathizing [with one another], loving [each other] as brethren [of one household], compassionate and courteous (tenderhearted and humble). Never return evil for evil or insult for insult (scolding, tongue-lashing, berating), but on the contrary blessing [praying for their welfare, happiness, and protection, and truly pitying and loving them]. For know that to this you have been called, that you may yourselves inherit a blessing [from God–that you may obtain a blessing as heirs, bringing welfare and happiness and protection].”

# Chapter 018 · 2026-06-02 · The Master Builder

Date: 2026-06-02 15:00:05
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=https://youtu.be/KNJN0irmQcs
URL: https://thelordsglorywithlori.com/daily-word/the-master-builder/

My children, it’s time to rise to the occasion. It’s time to remove the nephilim. My hand of judgement is about to strike. My wrath will be felt. You, My bride have nothing to fear. This is for the wicked on the earth. This is for those who chose to do wrong and cause harm. You’ll be like fresh seeds planted in good soil. You will produce much in the coming season. Fruitful, fruitful you will be for bringing in My SON’S Kingdom. The Golden Age is the prep work for My Kingdom to come. It’s part of a mystery I ordained long ago when I fashioned the world in its place. I hold it all in place. Nothing you see is by chance. It all plays an intricate part. You are My chosen vessels. This generation is who gets to bring them all in. You’ll soon have more than you ever dreamed.

A seed is planted. It’s time for My harvest. It’s time for revival on a global scale. It’s time for My church to excel. I’m breaking you free from all the chains that have held you bound unable to walk in My power and authority. I have a fresh new work I am doing on the earth. It’s more than you could possible ever think or imagine. It’s beyond your wildest dreams. You are about to witness it firsthand. All I promised is about to come in.

You are the first fruits of this new season. Your children and grand children will walk in your legacy. They will finish what you have started. They will walk in righteousness.

No more plowing the ground. Your lives are about to become much easier with all the new technology. Things will flow with ease. You are being transformed. This new age holds more than you know! What you sow is what you reap. The future generations will have this instilled in them. You are bringing up Kingdom minded children.

The Ring of Fire, pay close attention. It is about to light up. I have told you these things are coming. I am passing judgement on the world in this hour but My children are safe in My hands. I ordained every second of your life. If I can cloth the fields and take care of the ravens I can care for you. You are so dear to My heart.

Tectonic plates are shifting, I am moving mountains in India. I can dry up the Red Sea. A major change will take place in Haiti. I am a Master Builder and I am remodeling the earth. Much change will occur.

“A bruised reed He will not break, and a smoldering (dimly burning) wick He will not quench, till He brings justice and a just cause to victory.”

Matthew 12:20 AMPC

• The “Battered” or “Bruised” Reed:

A hollow stalk of grass or a reed by a river that is cracked, bent, or

crushed. In the ancient world, a damaged reed was useless; people would normally snap it completely and throw it away. The battered reed represents a person who is spiritually, emotionally, or physically crushed by sin, suffering, guilt, or life’s trials. Jesus does not finish off or discard the broken person; instead, He treats them with delicate care and restores them.

• The “Smoldering” or “Smoking”

Wick: The wick of an oil lamp where the flame has died out, leaving only a faint, smoking, flickering ember. It represents someone whose faith, hope, or life energy is dim, feeble, and barely hanging on. Jesus does not snuff out or extinguish that faint spark; instead, He protects and nurtures it until it burns bright again.

“Therefore, you are no longer a slave (bond servant) but a son; and if a son, then [it follows that you are] an heir by the aid of God, through Christ.”

Galatians 4:7

“He Who descended is the [very] same as He Who also has ascended high above all the heavens, that He [His presence] might fill all things (the whole universe, from the lowest to the highest). And His gifts were [varied; He Himself appointed and gave men to us] some to be apostles (special messengers), some prophets (inspired preachers and expounders), some evangelists (preachers of the Gospel, traveling missionaries), some pastors (shepherds of His flock) and teachers. His intention was the perfecting and the full equipping of the saints (His consecrated people), [that they should do] the work of ministering toward building up Christ’s body (the church), [That it might develop] until we all attain oneness in the faith and in the comprehension of the [full and accurate] knowledge of the Son of God, that [we might arrive] at really mature manhood (the completeness of personality which is nothing less than the standard height of Christ’s own perfection), the measure of the stature of the fullness of the Christ and the completeness found in Him. So then, we may no longer be children, tossed [like ships] to and fro between chance gusts of teaching and wavering with every changing wind of doctrine, [the prey of] the cunning and cleverness of unscrupulous men, [gamblers engaged] in every shifting form of trickery in inventing errors to mislead. Rather, let our lives lovingly express truth [in all things, speaking truly, dealing truly, living truly]. Enfolded in love, let us grow up in every way and in all things into Him Who is the Head, [even] Christ (the Messiah, the Anointed One). For because of Him the whole body (the church, in all its various parts), closely joined and firmly knit together by the joints and ligaments with which it is supplied, when each part [with power adapted to its need] is working properly [in all its functions], grows to full maturity, building itself up in love.”

Ephesians 4:10-16 AMPC

“The voice of the Lord is upon the waters; the God of glory thunders; the Lord is upon many (great) waters. The voice of the Lord is powerful; the voice of the Lord is full of majesty.”

“THEREFORE, [there is] now no condemnation (no adjudging guilty of wrong) for those who are in Christ Jesus, who live [and] walk not after the dictates of the flesh, but after the dictates of the Spirit. [John 3:18.] For the law of the Spirit of life [which is] in Christ Jesus [the law of our new being] has freed me from the law of sin and of death. For God has done what the Law could not do, [its power] being weakened by the flesh [the entire nature of man without the Holy Spirit]. Sending His own Son in the guise of sinful flesh and as an offering for sin, [God] condemned sin in the flesh [subdued, overcame, deprived it of its power over all who accept that sacrifice], [Lev. 7:37.] So that the righteous and just requirement of the Law might be fully met in us who live and move not in the ways of the flesh but in the ways of the Spirit [our lives governed not by the standards and according to the dictates of the flesh, but controlled by the Holy Spirit]. For those who are according to the flesh and are controlled by its unholy desires set their minds on and pursue those things which gratify the flesh, but those who are according to the Spirit and are controlled by the desires of the Spirit set their minds on and seek those things which gratify the [Holy] Spirit. Now the mind of the flesh [which is sense and reason without the Holy Spirit] is death [death that comprises all the miseries arising from sin, both here and hereafter]. But the mind of the [Holy] Spirit is life and [soul] peace [both now and forever]. [That is] because the mind of the flesh [with its carnal thoughts and purposes] is hostile to God, for it does not submit itself to God’s Law; indeed it cannot. So then those who are living the life of the flesh [catering to the appetites and impulses of their carnal nature] cannot please or satisfy God, or be acceptable to Him. But you are not living the life of the flesh, you are living the life of the Spirit, if the [Holy] Spirit of God [really] dwells within you [directs and controls you]. But if anyone does not possess the [Holy] Spirit of Christ, he is none of His [he does not belong to Christ, is not truly a child of God]. [Rom. 8:14.] But if Christ lives in you, [then although] your [natural] body is dead by reason of sin and guilt, the spirit is alive because of [the] righteousness [that He imputes to you]. And if the Spirit of Him Who raised up Jesus from the dead dwells in you, [then] He Who raised up Christ Jesus from the dead will also restore to life your mortal (short-lived, perishable) bodies through His Spirit Who dwells in you.”

Romans 8:1-11 AMPC

Love endures long and is patient and kind; love never is envious nor boils over with jealousy, is not boastful or vainglorious, does not display itself haughtily. It is not conceited (arrogant and inflated with pride); it is not rude (unmannerly) and does not act unbecomingly. Love (God’s love in us) does not insist on its own rights or its own way, for it is not self-seeking; it is not touchy or fretful or resentful; it takes no account of the evil done to it [it pays no attention to a suffered wrong]. It does not rejoice at injustice and unrighteousness, but rejoices when right and truth prevail. Love bears up under anything and everything that comes, is ever ready to believe the best of every person, its hopes are fadeless under all circumstances, and it endures everything [without weakening]. Love never fails [never fades out or becomes obsolete or comes to an end]. As for prophecy (the gift of interpreting the divine will and purpose), it will be fulfilled and pass away; as for tongues, they will be destroyed and cease; as for knowledge, it will pass away [it will lose its value and be superseded by truth]. For our knowledge is fragmentary (incomplete and imperfect), and our prophecy (our teaching) is fragmentary (incomplete and imperfect). But when the complete and perfect (total) comes, the incomplete and imperfect will vanish away (become antiquated, void, and superseded). When I was a child, I talked like a child, I thought like a child, I reasoned like a child; now that I have become a man, I am done with childish ways and have put them aside. For now we are looking in a mirror that gives only a dim (blurred) reflection [of reality as in a riddle or enigma], but then [when perfection comes] we shall see in reality and face to face! Now I know in part (imperfectly), but then I shall know and understand fully and clearly, even in the same manner as I have been fully and clearly known and understood [by God]. And so faith, hope, love abide [faith–conviction and belief respecting man’s relation to God and divine things; hope–joyful and confident expectation of eternal salvation; love–true affection for God and man, growing out of God’s love for and in us], these three; but the greatest of these is love.”

# Chapter 098 · 2026-10-01 · Refiners Fire

“JUDE, A servant of Jesus Christ (the Messiah), and brother of James, [writes this letter] to those who are called (chosen), dearly loved by God the Father and separated (set apart) and kept for Jesus Christ: May mercy, [soul] peace, and love be multiplied to you. Beloved, my whole concern was to write to you in regard to our common salvation. [But] I found it necessary and was impelled to write you and urgently appeal to and exhort [you] to contend for the faith which was once for all handed down to the saints [the faith which is that sum of Christian belief which was delivered verbally to the holy people of God]. For certain men have crept in stealthily [gaining entrance secretly by a side door]. Their doom was predicted long ago, ungodly (impious, profane) persons who pervert the grace (the spiritual blessing and favor) of our God into lawlessness and wantonness and immorality, and disown and deny our sole Master and Lord, Jesus Christ (the Messiah, the Anointed One). Now I want to remind you, though you were fully informed once for all, that though the Lord [at one time] delivered a people out of the land of Egypt, He subsequently destroyed those [of them] who did not believe [who refused to adhere to, trust in, and rely upon Him]. And angels who did not keep (care for, guard, and hold to) their own first place of power but abandoned their proper dwelling place–these He has reserved in custody in eternal chains (bonds) under the thick gloom of utter darkness until the judgment and doom of the great day. [The wicked are sentenced to suffer] just as Sodom and Gomorrah and the adjacent towns–which likewise gave themselves over to impurity and indulged in unnatural vice and sensual perversity–are laid out [in plain sight] as an exhibit of perpetual punishment [to warn] of everlasting fire. [Gen. 19.] Nevertheless in like manner, these dreamers also corrupt the body, scorn and reject authority and government, and revile and libel and scoff at [heavenly] glories (the glorious ones). But when [even] the archangel Michael, contending with the devil, judicially argued (disputed) about the body of Moses, he dared not [presume to] bring an abusive condemnation against him, but [simply] said, The Lord rebuke you! [Zech. 3:2.] But these men revile (scoff and sneer at) anything they do not happen to be acquainted with and do not understand; and whatever they do understand physically [that which they know by mere instinct], like irrational beasts–by these they corrupt themselves and are destroyed (perish). Woe to them! For they have run riotously in the way of Cain, and have abandoned themselves for the sake of gain [it offers them, following] the error of Balaam, and have perished in rebellion [like that] of Korah! [Gen. 4:3-8; Num. 16; 22-24.] These are hidden reefs (elements of danger) in your love feasts, where they boldly feast sumptuously [carousing together in your midst], without scruples providing for themselves [alone]. They are clouds without water, swept along by the winds; trees, without fruit at the late autumn gathering time–twice (doubly) dead, [lifeless and] plucked up by the roots; Wild waves of the sea, flinging up the foam of their own shame and disgrace; wandering stars, for whom the gloom of eternal darkness has been reserved forever. It was of these people, moreover, that Enoch in the seventh [generation] from Adam prophesied when he said, Behold, the Lord comes with His myriads of holy ones (ten thousands of His saints) To execute judgment upon all and to convict all the impious (unholy ones) of all their ungodly deeds which they have committed [in such an] ungodly [way], and of all the severe (abusive, jarring) things which ungodly sinners have spoken against Him. These are inveterate murmurers (grumblers) who complain [of their lot in life], going after their own desires [controlled by their passions]; their talk is boastful and arrogant, [and they claim to] admire men’s persons and pay people flattering compliments to gain advantage. But you must remember, beloved, the predictions which were made by the apostles (the special messengers) of our Lord Jesus Christ (the Messiah, the Anointed One). They told you beforehand, In the last days (in the end time) there will be scoffers [who seek to gratify their own unholy desires], following after their own ungodly passions. It is these who are [agitators] setting up distinctions and causing divisions–merely sensual [creatures, carnal, worldly-minded people], devoid of the [Holy] Spirit and destitute of any higher spiritual life. But you, beloved, build yourselves up [founded] on your most holy faith [make progress, rise like an edifice higher and higher], praying in the Holy Spirit; Guard and keep yourselves in the love of God; expect and patiently wait for the mercy of our Lord Jesus Christ (the Messiah)–[which will bring you] unto life eternal. And refute [so as to] convict some who dispute with you, and on some have mercy who waver and doubt. [Strive to] save others, snatching [them] out of [the] fire; on others take pity [but] with fear, loathing even the garment spotted by the flesh and polluted by their sensuality. [Zech. 3:2-4.] Now to Him Who is able to keep you without stumbling or slipping or falling, and to present [you] unblemished (blameless and faultless) before the presence of His glory in triumphant joy and exultation [with unspeakable, ecstatic delight]– To the one only God, our Savior through Jesus Christ our Lord, be glory (splendor), majesty, might and dominion, and power and authority, before all time and now and forever (unto all the ages of eternity). Amen (so be it).”

Jude 1:1-25 AMPC

“THEREFORE THEN, since we are surrounded by so great a cloud of witnesses [who have borne testimony to the Truth], let us strip off and throw aside every encumbrance (unnecessary weight) and that sin which so readily (deftly and cleverly) clings to and entangles us, and let us run with patient endurance and steady and active persistence the appointed course of the race that is set before us, Looking away [from all that will distract] to Jesus, Who is the Leader and the Source of our faith [giving the first incentive for our belief] and is also its Finisher [bringing it to maturity and perfection]. He, for the joy [of obtaining the prize] that was set before Him, endured the cross, despising and ignoring the shame, and is now seated at the right hand of the throne of God. [Ps. 110:1.] Just think of Him Who endured from sinners such grievous opposition and bitter hostility against Himself [reckon up and consider it all in comparison with your trials], so that you may not grow weary or exhausted, losing heart and relaxing and fainting in your minds. You have not yet struggled and fought agonizingly against sin, nor have you yet resisted and withstood to the point of pouring out your [own] blood. And have you [completely] forgotten the divine word of appeal and encouragement in which you are reasoned with and addressed as sons? My son, do not think lightly or scorn to submit to the correction and discipline of the Lord, nor lose courage and give up and faint when you are reproved or corrected by Him; For the Lord corrects and disciplines everyone whom He loves, and He punishes, even scourges, every son whom He accepts and welcomes to His heart and cherishes. You must submit to and endure [correction] for discipline; God is dealing with you as with sons. For what son is there whom his father does not [thus] train and correct and discipline? Now if you are exempt from correction and left without discipline in which all [of God’s children] share, then you are illegitimate offspring and not true sons [at all]. [Prov. 3:11, 12.] Moreover, we have had earthly fathers who disciplined us and we yielded [to them] and respected [them for training us]. Shall we not much more cheerfully submit to the Father of spirits and so [truly] live? For [our earthly fathers] disciplined us for only a short period of time and chastised us as seemed proper and good to them; but He disciplines us for our certain good, that we may become sharers in His own holiness. For the time being no discipline brings joy, but seems grievous and painful; but afterwards it yields a peaceable fruit of righteousness to those who have been trained by it [a harvest of fruit which consists in righteousness–in conformity to God’s will in purpose, thought, and action, resulting in right living and right standing with God]. So then, brace up and reinvigorate and set right your slackened and weakened and drooping hands and strengthen your feeble and palsied and tottering knees, [Isa. 35:3.] And cut through and make firm and plain and smooth, straight paths for your feet [yes, make them safe and upright and happy paths that go in the right direction], so that the lame and halting [limbs] may not be put out of joint, but rather may be cured. Strive to live in peace with everybody and pursue that consecration and holiness without which no one will [ever] see the Lord. Exercise foresight and be on the watch to look [after one another], to see that no one falls back from and fails to secure God’s grace (His unmerited favor and spiritual blessing), in order that no root of resentment (rancor, bitterness, or hatred) shoots forth and causes trouble and bitter torment, and the many become contaminated and defiled by it– That no one may become guilty of sexual vice, or become a profane (godless and sacrilegious) person as Esau did, who sold his own birthright for a single meal. [Gen. 25:29-34.] For you understand that later on, when he wanted [to regain title to] his inheritance of the blessing, he was rejected (disqualified and set aside), for he could find no opportunity to repair by repentance [what he had done, no chance to recall the choice he had made], although he sought for it carefully with [bitter] tears. [Gen. 27:30-40.] For you have not come [as did the Israelites in the wilderness] to a [material] mountain that can be touched, [a mountain] that is ablaze with fire, and to gloom and darkness and a raging storm, And to the blast of a trumpet and a voice whose words make the listeners beg that nothing more be said to them. [Exod. 19:12-22; 20:18-21; Deut. 4:11, 12; 5:22-27.] For they could not bear the command that was given: If even a wild animal touches the mountain, it shall be stoned to death. [Exod. 19:12, 13.] In fact, so awful and terrifying was the [phenomenal] sight that Moses said, I am terrified (aghast and trembling with fear). [Deut. 9:19.] But rather, you have come to Mount Zion, even to the city of the living God, the heavenly Jerusalem, and to countless multitudes of angels in festal gathering, And to the church (assembly) of the Firstborn who are registered [as citizens] in heaven, and to the God Who is Judge of all, and to the spirits of the righteous (the redeemed in heaven) who have been made perfect, And to Jesus, the Mediator (Go-between, Agent) of a new covenant, and to the sprinkled blood which speaks [of mercy], a better and nobler and more gracious message than the blood of Abel [which cried out for vengeance]. [Gen. 4:10.] So see to it that you do not reject Him or refuse to listen to and heed Him Who is speaking [to you now]. For if they [the Israelites] did not escape when they refused to listen and heed Him Who warned and divinely instructed them [here] on earth [revealing with heavenly warnings His will], how much less shall we escape if we reject and turn our backs on Him Who cautions and admonishes [us] from heaven? Then [at Mount Sinai] His voice shook the earth, but now He has given a promise: Yet once more I will shake and make tremble not only the earth but also the [starry] heavens. [Hag. 2:6.] Now this expression, Yet once more, indicates the final removal and transformation of all [that can be] shaken–that is, of that which has been created–in order that what cannot be shaken may remain and continue. [Ps. 102:26.] Let us therefore, receiving a kingdom that is firm and stable and cannot be shaken, offer to God pleasing service and acceptable worship, with modesty and pious care and godly fear and awe; For our God [is indeed] a consuming fire. [Deut. 4:24.]”

Hebrews 12:1-29 AMPC

HEB. 13:5,6 AMPL

“Let your character or moral disposition be free from love of money [including greed, avarice, lust, and craving for earthly possessions] and be satisfied with your present [circumstances and with what you have]; for He [God] Himself has said, I will not in any way fail you nor give you up nor leave you without support. [I will] not, [I will] not, [I will] not in any degree leave you helpless nor forsake nor let [you] down (relax My hold on you)! [Assuredly not!] [Josh. 1:5.] So we take comfort and are encouraged and confidently and boldly say, The Lord is my Helper; I will not be seized with alarm [I will not fear or dread or be terrified]. What can man do to me? [Ps. 27:1;118:6.]”

But the Lord made Pharaoh’s heart stronger and more stubborn, and he would not let them go. And Pharaoh said to Moses, Get away from me! Exodus 10:21-29 AMPC

“AND THE Lord said to Moses, Tell the Israelites to turn back and encamp before Pi-hahiroth, between Migdol and the [Red] Sea, before Baal-zephon. You shall encamp opposite it by the sea. For Pharaoh will say of the Israelites, They are entangled in the land; the wilderness has shut them in. I will harden (make stubborn, strong) Pharaoh’s heart, that he will pursue them, and I will gain honor and glory over Pharaoh and all his host, and the Egyptians shall know that I am the Lord. And they did so. It was told the king of Egypt that the people had fled; and the heart of Pharaoh and of his servants was changed toward the people, and they said, What is this we have done? We have let Israel go from serving us! And he made ready his chariots and took his army, And took 600 chosen chariots and all the other chariots of Egypt, with officers over all of them. The Lord made hard and strong the heart of Pharaoh king of Egypt, and he pursued the Israelites, for [they] left proudly and defiantly. [Acts 13:17.] The Egyptians pursued them, all the horses and chariots of Pharaoh and his horsemen and his army, and overtook them encamped at the [Red] Sea by Pi-hahiroth, in front of Baal-zephon. When Pharaoh drew near, the Israelites looked up, and behold, the Egyptians were marching after them; and the Israelites were exceedingly frightened and cried out to the Lord. And they said to Moses, Is it because there are no graves in Egypt that you have taken us away to die in the wilderness? Why have you treated us this way and brought us out of Egypt? Did we not tell you in Egypt, Let us alone; let us serve the Egyptians? For it would have been better for us to serve the Egyptians than to die in the wilderness. Moses told the people, Fear not; stand still (firm, confident, undismayed) and see the salvation of the Lord which He will work for you today. For the Egyptians you have seen today you shall never see again. The Lord will fight for you, and you shall hold your peace and remain at rest. The Lord said to Moses, Why do you cry to Me? Tell the people of Israel to go forward! Lift up your rod and stretch out your hand over the sea and divide it, and the Israelites shall go on dry ground through the midst of the sea. And I, behold, I will harden (make stubborn and strong) the hearts of the Egyptians, and they shall go [into the sea] after them; and I will gain honor over Pharaoh and all his host, his chariots, and horsemen. The Egyptians shall know and realize that I am the Lord when I have gained honor and glory over Pharaoh, his chariots, and his horsemen. And the Angel of God Who went before the host of Israel moved and went behind them; and the pillar of the cloud went from before them and stood behind them, Coming between the host of Egypt and the host of Israel. It was a cloud and darkness to the Egyptians, but it gave light by night to the Israelites; and the one host did not come near the other all night. Then Moses stretched out his hand over the sea, and the Lord caused the sea to go back by a strong east wind all that night and made the sea dry land; and the waters were divided. And the Israelites went into the midst of the sea on dry ground, the waters being a wall to them on their right hand and on their left. The Egyptians pursued and went in after them into the midst of the sea, even all Pharaoh’s horses, his chariots, and his horsemen. And in the morning watch the Lord through the pillar of fire and cloud looked down on the host of the Egyptians and discomfited [them], And bound (clogged, took off) their chariot wheels, making them drive heavily; and the Egyptians said, Let us flee from the face of Israel, for the Lord fights for them against the Egyptians! So Moses stretched forth his hand over the sea, and the sea returned to its strength and normal flow when the morning appeared; and the Egyptians fled into it [being met by it]; and the Lord overthrew the Egyptians and shook them off into the midst of the sea. The waters returned and covered the chariots, the horsemen, and all the host of Pharaoh that pursued them; not even one of them remained. But the Israelites walked on dry ground in the midst of the sea, the waters being a wall to them on their right hand and on their left. Thus the Lord saved Israel that day from the hand of the Egyptians, and Israel saw the Egyptians dead upon the seashore. And Israel saw that great work which the Lord did against the Egyptians, and the people [reverently] feared the Lord and trusted in (relied on, remained steadfast to) the Lord and to His servant Moses.”

Exodus 14:1-25, 27-31 AMPC
